# P330 — 2026-11-10 ~10:30 KST — Reject ambiguous duplicate DOCX package parts; exact SHA CI SUCCESS; 82/100; PDF FROZEN

1. **Purpose/confirmed requirements/technology.** KUODRA backend-only continues on owner instruction `ㄱ`; preserve public/private canonicals, SEC A/B/C, Guard, ML, evidence and local reviews. Python 3.11, python-docx, ZIP stdlib, Streamlit/SQLite/sklearn. User deferred preliminary PDF until final stage. DAKER preliminary deadline 2026-11-12 10:00 KST; no contest submission.
2. **Completed feature and architectural decision.** Local document adapter `_safe_docx_table` previously checked ZIP traversal, active content, encryption, item count and decompression sizes but allowed duplicate ZIP member filenames. Reproduced with synthetically generated valid DOCX: insert a second `word/document.xml` with entity `issuerY` while original had `issuerA`; python-docx reads the LAST entry and a duplicate before the original reads `issuerA`. Thus two different financial measurements can be embedded under the same OPC package-part name; hash by itself does not resolve source ambiguity. Approach A: select a first/last part and record precedence (simple but hides contradictory source bytes). Approach B: **reject duplicate part names upfront with O(n) set check**, no new dependency, no silent source interpretation. Selected B before reading relationship parts/parser. Added test duplicates both first and last, expects `DOCUMENT_DOCX_PACKAGE_DENIED` while standard DOCX normalization remains supported.
3. **Verification.** Verified exact feature+test commit `31c8501eff525f07a187f1ec247b5a8213fa1978`, [GitHub Actions #42735714772](https://github.com/hibi89/hibi89-kic-proofinvest/actions/runs/42735714772) **attempt1 COMPLETED SUCCESS**. # P329 — 2026-11-10 10:03 KST — Encoded external DOCX relationships now rejected before local parsing; CI SUCCESS; 82/100; PDF FROZEN

1. **Goal, product scope, technology.** Owner's `rㄱ` continues KUODRA backend-only development; do not modify preliminary PDF until final backend work is independently complete or externally blocked. Owner-scoped canonical PUBLIC/PRIVATE SEC/financial source evidence, guarded A/B/C, exploratory ML and human report; Python 3.11, SQLite, Streamlit, sklearn, python-docx, built-in XML parser. KIC DAKER submission due **2026-11-12 10:00 KST**, owner-only official account action.
2. **Concrete flaw/design decision/correction.** In `proofinvest/kuodra_document_ingest.py`, `_safe_docx_table` inspected DOCX `.rels` for the literal regex `TargetMode="External"`. Reproduction with a valid fictional DOCX and an unused hyperlink relationship `TargetMode="&#x45;xternal"`: the regex missed it, XML parsed to **External**, and python-docx loaded the document; the explicit source policy to reject external relationships was bypassed. Two approaches: extend regex for encodings (brittle, many XML entity forms) vs parse bounded relationship XML using standard-library `ElementTree` (same bounded local scan, no API/extra package, robust entity decoding).
